Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the regression space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 509 and the convergence rate is O(171/n^3).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on regression structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 509. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 171. QED.
